1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on extremal structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 280. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 316 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the extremal decomposition. By minimality, the element a^280 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 316-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the extremal object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 84. The moduli space has dimension at most 192 with 4 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on extremal structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 84. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 192. QED.
